We claim that \\\\(\\\\alpha\\\\) and \\\\(\\\\beta\\\\) are not coprime in \\\\(\\\\Bbb Z[i]\\\\).  Indeed, if \\\\(\\\\gcd(\\\\alpha,\\\\beta)=1\\\\) then, up to units, the prime factors of \\\\(\\\\alpha\\\\) together with those of \\\\(\\\\overline\\\\alpha\\\\) would partition the prime factors of \\\\(e^5\\\\) into two disjoint subsets of equal total multiplicity.
